            <script>\n
    (() => {\n
        const shell = document.querySelector('[data-admin-shell]');\n
        if (!shell) return;\n
        const sidebar = shell.querySelector('[data-admin-sidebar]');\n
        const toggle = shell.querySelector('[data-admin-toggle]');\n
        const backdrop = shell.querySelector('[data-admin-backdrop]');\n
        const closeSidebar = () => {\n
            sidebar?.classList.remove('is-open');\n
            backdrop?.classList.remove('is-visible');\n
        };\n
        toggle?.addEventListener('click', () => {\n
            sidebar?.classList.toggle('is-open');\n
            backdrop?.classList.toggle('is-visible');\n
        });\n
        backdrop?.addEventListener('click', closeSidebar);\n
        sidebar?.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(a => a.addEventListener('click', closeSidebar));\n
\n
        // Preserve the independent sidebar position between admin page navigations.\n
        // The active route is still rendered server-side; this only restores/reveals it\n
        // inside the sidebar scroll container and never touches the main workspace.\n
        const sidebarNav = shell.querySelector('.admin-sidebar-nav-scroll');\n
        const sidebarScrollKey = 'yza_admin_sidebar_scroll_top';\n
        if (sidebarNav && sidebarNav.dataset.smartScrollReady !== '1') {\n
            sidebarNav.dataset.smartScrollReady = '1';\n
            let saveTimer = null;\n
            const writeSidebarPosition = () => {\n
                try { window.sessionStorage.setItem(sidebarScrollKey, String(Math.max(0, sidebarNav.scrollTop))); } catch (_) {}\n
            };\n
            const saveSidebarPosition = () => {\n
                window.clearTimeout(saveTimer);\n
                saveTimer = window.setTimeout(writeSidebarPosition, 80);\n
            };\n
            const clamp = (value) => Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(value) || 0, sidebarNav.scrollHeight - sidebarNav.clientHeight));\n
            const activeItem = sidebarNav.querySelector('.admin-nav-item.is-active');\n
            const revealActiveItemIfNeeded = () => {\n
                if (!activeItem || sidebarNav.clientHeight <= 0) return;\n
                const navRect = sidebarNav.getBoundingClientRect();\n
                const itemRect = activeItem.getBoundingClientRect();\n
                const edgePadding = 12;\n
                if (itemRect.top < navRect.top + edgePadding) {\n
                    sidebarNav.scrollTop = clamp(sidebarNav.scrollTop + itemRect.top - navRect.top - edgePadding);\n
                } else if (itemRect.bottom > navRect.bottom - edgePadding) {\n
                    sidebarNav.scrollTop = clamp(sidebarNav.scrollTop + itemRect.bottom - navRect.bottom + edgePadding);\n
                }\n
            };\n
            const restoreSidebarPosition = () => {\n
                let storedPosition = null;\n
                try { storedPosition = window.sessionStorage.getItem(sidebarScrollKey); } catch (_) {}\n
                if (storedPosition !== null) sidebarNav.scrollTop = clamp(storedPosition);\n
                revealActiveItemIfNeeded();\n
            };\n
            sidebarNav.addEventListener('scroll', saveSidebarPosition, { passive: true });\n
            sidebar?.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(link => link.addEventListener('click', writeSidebarPosition));\n
            window.addEventListener('pagehide', writeSidebarPosition, { once: true });\n
            window.requestAnimationFrame(() => window.requestAnimationFrame(restoreSidebarPosition));\n
        }\n
\n
        const sidebarCollapseToggle = shell.querySelector('[data-admin-sidebar-collapse]');\n
        const sidebarCollapseKey = 'yza_admin_sidebar_collapsed';\n
        const desktopSidebarQuery = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1025px)');\n
        const readCollapsedState = () => {\n
            try { return window.localStorage.getItem(sidebarCollapseKey) === '1'; } catch (_) { return false; }\n
        };\n
        const writeCollapsedState = (collapsed) => {\n
            try { window.localStorage.setItem(sidebarCollapseKey, collapsed ? '1' : '0'); } catch (_) {}\n
        };\n
        const setSidebarCollapsed = (collapsed, persist = false) => {\n
            const effectiveCollapsed = desktopSidebarQuery.matches && collapsed;\n
            shell.classList.toggle('is-sidebar-collapsed', effectiveCollapsed);\n
            sidebarCollapseToggle?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', effectiveCollapsed ? 'false' : 'true');\n
            sidebarCollapseToggle?.setAttribute('aria-label', effectiveCollapsed ? 'Perluas sidebar' : 'Ciutkan sidebar');\n
            if (sidebarCollapseToggle) sidebarCollapseToggle.title = effectiveCollapsed ? 'Perluas sidebar' : 'Ciutkan sidebar';\n
            if (persist && desktopSidebarQuery.matches) writeCollapsedState(effectiveCollapsed);\n
            if (!effectiveCollapsed && sidebarNav) window.requestAnimationFrame(() => window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n
                const active = sidebarNav.querySelector('.admin-nav-item.is-active');\n
                if (active) {\n
                    const navRect = sidebarNav.getBoundingClientRect();\n
                    const itemRect = active.getBoundingClientRect();\n
                    const padding = 12;\n
                    if (itemRect.top < navRect.top + padding) sidebarNav.scrollTop = Math.max(0, sidebarNav.scrollTop + itemRect.top - navRect.top - padding);\n
                    else if (itemRect.bottom > navRect.bottom - padding) sidebarNav.scrollTop = Math.min(sidebarNav.scrollHeight - sidebarNav.clientHeight, sidebarNav.scrollTop + itemRect.bottom - navRect.bottom + padding);\n
                }\n
            }));\n
        };\n
        setSidebarCollapsed(readCollapsedState());\n
        sidebarCollapseToggle?.addEventListener('click', () => setSidebarCollapsed(!shell.classList.contains('is-sidebar-collapsed'), true));\n
        desktopSidebarQuery.addEventListener?.('change', () => setSidebarCollapsed(readCollapsedState()));\n
\n
        // User dropdown menu\n
        const userMenu = shell.querySelector('[data-admin-user-menu]');\n
        const userToggle = userMenu?.querySelector('[data-admin-user-toggle]');\n
        const userDropdown = userMenu?.querySelector('[data-admin-dropdown]');\n
        const closeUserMenu = () => {\n
            if (userDropdown) userDropdown.hidden = true;\n
            userToggle?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');\n
        };\n
        userToggle?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {\n
            e.stopPropagation();\n
            const isHidden = userDropdown?.hidden ?? true;\n
            if (userDropdown) userDropdown.hidden = !isHidden;\n
            userToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isHidden ? 'true' : 'false');\n
        });\n
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {\n
            if (userMenu && !userMenu.contains(e.target)) closeUserMenu();\n
        });\n
\n
        // Quick search focus (Ctrl+K or Cmd+K)\n
        const searchInput = shell.querySelector('[data-admin-quick-search]');\n
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {\n
            if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.key === 'k' || e.key === 'K')) {\n
                e.preventDefault();\n
                searchInput?.focus();\n
            }\n
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
                closeUserMenu();\n
                closeSidebar();\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
    })();\n
    </script>\n
        <script>\n
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {\n
    // -------------------------------------------------------------\n
    // TAB SWITCHING (INVENTORI / KATALOG / FOTO)\n
    // -------------------------------------------------------------\n
    const tabButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.vehicle-tab-btn');\n
    const tabPanes = document.querySelectorAll('.vehicle-tab-pane');\n
\n
    function activateTab(tabId, updateUrl = true) {\n
        tabButtons.forEach(btn => {\n
            const isActive = btn.getAttribute('data-tab-target') === tabId;\n
            btn.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);\n
            btn.setAttribute('aria-selected', isActive ? 'true' : 'false');\n
        });\n
\n
        tabPanes.forEach(pane => {\n
            const isActive = pane.id === tabId;\n
            pane.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);\n
        });\n
\n
        if (updateUrl) {\n
            const shortName = tabId.replace('tab-', '');\n
            const url = new URL(window.location.href);\n
            url.searchParams.set('tab', shortName);\n
            window.history.replaceState({}, '', url.toString());\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    tabButtons.forEach(btn => {\n
        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
            const targetId = this.getAttribute('data-tab-target');\n
            activateTab(targetId, true);\n
        });\n
    });\n
\n
    const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);\n
    const preorderContext = Number(urlParams.get('preorder'));\n
    if (Number.isInteger(preorderContext) && preorderContext > 0) {\n
        const back = document.createElement('a');\n
        back.className = 'btn-modal-secondary'; back.textContent = '← Kembali ke Pre-Order';\n
        back.href = 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/admin/pre-order-mobil?review=' + preorderContext;\n

        activateTab('tab-' + queryTab, false);\n
    }\n

\n
    function renderDropdownItems(car) {\n
        if (!floatingMenu) return;\n
        const isVirtual = Boolean(car.is_virtual);\n
        const poCount = Array.isArray(car.preorders) ? car.preorders.length : 0;\n
        const canManage = !isUserOwner;\n

        let html = '';\n

        if (!isVirtual) {\n
            // A. MOBIL READY / MEMILIKI UNIT FISIK\n

                <button type="button" class="dropdown-item" data-action="detail" role="menuitem">\n

                    <span>Detail Kendaraan</span>\n
                </button>\n
            `;\n
\n
            // 2. Kelola Stok (Hanya Staf Berwenang)\n
            if (canManage) {\n

                        <span>Kelola Stok</span>\n
                    </button>\n
                `;\n
            }\n
\n
            // 3. Edit Kendaraan (Hanya Staf Berwenang)\n
            if (canManage) {\n
                html += `\n
                    <button type="button" class="dropdown-item" data-action="edit" role="menuitem">\n
                        <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M17 3a2.828 2.828 0 1 1 4 4L7.5 20.5 2 22l1.5-5.5L17 3z"></path></svg>\n
                        <span>Edit Kendaraan</span>\n
                    </button>\n
                `;\n
            }\n
\n
            // 4. Galeri & Warna\n
            html += `\n
                <button type="button" class="dropdown-item" data-action="gallery" role="menuitem">\n
                    <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 2a14.5 14.5 0 0 0 0 20 14.5 14.5 0 0 0 0-20"></path></svg>\n
                    <span>Galeri &amp; Warna</span>\n
                </button>\n
            `;\n
\n
            // 5. Permintaan Pre-Order (Hanya bila jumlah aktif > 0)\n
            if (poCount > 0) {\n
                html += `\n
                    <button type="button" class="dropdown-item has-badge" data-action="preorder" role="menuitem">\n
                        <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M12 8v4l3 3"></path><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"></circle></svg>\n
                        <span>Permintaan Pre-Order</span>\n
                        <span class="dropdown-badge">${poCount}</span>\n
                    </button>\n
                `;\n
            }\n
\n
            // 6. Nonaktifkan Mobil (Hanya jika diizinkan dan mobil belum terjual)\n
            if (canManage && car.status !== 'terjual') {\n
                html += `\n
                    <div class="dropdown-divider"></div>\n
                    <button type="button" class="dropdown-item is-danger" data-action="deactivate" role="menuitem">\n
                        <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="15" y1="9" x2="9" y2="15"></line><line x1="9" y1="9" x2="15" y2="15"></line></svg>\n
                        <span>Nonaktifkan Mobil</span>\n
                    </button>\n
                `;\n
            }\n
        } else {\n
            // B. MOBIL TANPA UNIT FISIK (KATALOG SHOWROOM)\n
            // 1. Detail Katalog\n
            html += `\n
                <button type="button" class="dropdown-item" data-action="detail" role="menuitem">\n
                    <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M1 12s4-8 11-8 11 8 11 8-4 8-11 8-11-8-11-8z"></path><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"></circle></svg>\n
                    <span>Detail Katalog</span>\n

            // 2. Tambah Unit Fisik (Hanya Staf Berwenang)\n
            if (canManage) {\n
                html += `\n
                    <button type="button" class="dropdown-item" data-action="add_unit" role="menuitem">\n
                        <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19"></line><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"></line></svg>\n
                        <span>Tambah Unit Fisik</span>\n
                    </button>\n
                `;\n
            }\n
\n
            // 3. Permintaan Pre-Order (N) jika terdapat permintaan aktif\n
            if (poCount > 0) {\n
                html += `\n
                    <button type="button" class="dropdown-item has-badge" data-action="preorder" role="menuitem">\n
                        <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M12 8v4l3 3"></path><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"></circle></svg>\n

            // 4. Edit Katalog (Hanya Staf Berwenang)\n

                        <span>Edit Katalog</span>\n
                    </button>\n
                `;\n
            }\n
\n
            // 5. Galeri & Warna\n
            html += `\n
                <button type="button" class="dropdown-item" data-action="gallery" role="menuitem">\n
                    <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><path d="M12 2a14.5 14.5 0 0 0 0 20 14.5 14.5 0 0 0 0-20"></path></svg>\n
                    <span>Galeri &amp; Warna</span>\n
                </button>\n
            `;\n
\n
            // 6. Nonaktifkan Model (Hanya Staf Berwenang)\n
            if (canManage) {\n
                html += `\n
                    <div class="dropdown-divider"></div>\n
                    <button type="button" class="dropdown-item is-danger" data-action="deactivate" role="menuitem">\n
                        <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="15" y1="9" x2="9" y2="15"></line><line x1="9" y1="9" x2="15" y2="15"></line></svg>\n
                        <span>Nonaktifkan Model</span>\n
                    </button>\n
                `;\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        floatingMenu.innerHTML = html;\n
\n
        // Pasang event listener untuk membuka modal\n
        floatingMenu.querySelectorAll('.dropdown-item').forEach(item => {\n
            item.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
                e.stopPropagation();\n
                const action = this.getAttribute('data-action');\n
                const targetCarId = currentDropdownCarId;\n
                hideFloatingDropdown();\n
                if (targetCarId && action) {\n
                    openVehicleActionModal(targetCarId, action);\n
                }\n
            });\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    window.toggleDropdownFixed = function (button, carId) {\n
        if (!floatingMenu) return;\n
\n
        if (!floatingMenu.hasAttribute('hidden') && currentDropdownCarId === carId) {\n
            hideFloatingDropdown();\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        const car = getCarPayload(carId);\n
        if (!car) {\n
            console.warn('Vehicle payload not found for ID:', carId);\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        currentDropdownCarId = carId;\n
        currentDropdownTriggerBtn = button;\n
\n
        renderDropdownItems(car);\n
\n
        floatingMenu.removeAttribute('hidden');\n
        button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');\n
\n
        const rect = button.getBoundingClientRect();\n
        const menuWidth = floatingMenu.offsetWidth || 230;\n
        const menuHeight = floatingMenu.offsetHeight || 220;\n
\n
        let left = rect.right - menuWidth;\n
        if (left < 10) left = 10;\n
        if (left + menuWidth > window.innerWidth - 10) {\n
            left = window.innerWidth - menuWidth - 10;\n
        }\n
\n
        let top = rect.bottom + 6;\n
        let isFlipped = false;\n
        if (top + menuHeight > window.innerHeight - 10) {\n
            top = rect.top - menuHeight - 6;\n
            isFlipped = true;\n
            if (top < 10) top = 10;\n
        }\n
\n
        floatingMenu.style.top = top + 'px';\n
        floatingMenu.style.left = left + 'px';\n
        floatingMenu.classList.toggle('is-flipped', isFlipped);\n
\n
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n
            floatingMenu.classList.add('is-open');\n
        });\n
    };\n
\n
    function hideFloatingDropdown() {\n
        if (!floatingMenu) return;\n
        floatingMenu.classList.remove('is-open');\n
        floatingMenu.setAttribute('hidden', '');\n
        if (currentDropdownTriggerBtn) {\n
            currentDropdownTriggerBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');\n
            currentDropdownTriggerBtn = null;\n
        }\n
        currentDropdownCarId = null;\n
    }\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
        if (floatingMenu && !floatingMenu.hasAttribute('hidden')) {\n
            if (!floatingMenu.contains(e.target) && !e.target.closest('.btn-more-options')) {\n
                hideFloatingDropdown();\n
            }\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {\n
        if (!floatingMenu || floatingMenu.hasAttribute('hidden')) return;\n
\n
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
            const btn = currentDropdownTriggerBtn;\n
            hideFloatingDropdown();\n
            if (btn) btn.focus();\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        const items = Array.from(floatingMenu.querySelectorAll('.dropdown-item'));\n
        if (!items.length) return;\n
\n
        const currentIndex = items.indexOf(document.activeElement);\n
\n
        if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {\n
            e.preventDefault();\n
            const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % items.length;\n
            items[nextIndex].focus();\n
        } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {\n
            e.preventDefault();\n
            const prevIndex = (currentIndex - 1 + items.length) % items.length;\n
            items[prevIndex].focus();\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    window.addEventListener('resize', hideFloatingDropdown);\n
    window.addEventListener('scroll', hideFloatingDropdown, true);\n
\n
    // Check All Checkbox\n
    const checkAll = document.getElementById('checkAllInventory');\n
    const rowChecks = document.querySelectorAll('.inventory-row-check');\n
    if (checkAll) {\n
        checkAll.addEventListener('change', function () {\n
            rowChecks.forEach(cb => cb.checked = checkAll.checked);\n
        });\n
    }\n
});\n

// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
// REUSABLE VEHICLE MANAGEMENT ACTION MODALS ENGINE\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
let activeModalTriggerElement = null;\n
const isUserOwner = false;\n
let isModalFormDirty = false;\n
\n
function resetModalFormDirty() {\n
    isModalFormDirty = false;\n
}\n
\n
function markModalFormDirty() {\n
    isModalFormDirty = true;\n
}\n
\n
function setupFormDirtyTracking(form) {\n
    if (!form) return;\n
    form.addEventListener('input', () => { isModalFormDirty = true; });\n
    form.addEventListener('change', () => { isModalFormDirty = true; });\n
}\n
\n
function getCarPayload(carId) {\n
    const scriptEl = document.getElementById('car-data-' + carId);\n
    if (scriptEl) {\n
        try {\n
            return JSON.parse(scriptEl.textContent);\n
        } catch (e) {\n
            console.error('Failed to parse car payload', e);\n
        }\n
    }\n
    return null;\n
}\n
\n
function updateLocalCarPayload(carId, updatedData) {\n
    const scriptEl = document.getElementById('car-data-' + carId);\n
    if (scriptEl) {\n
        const current = getCarPayload(carId) || {};\n
        const merged = Object.assign({}, current, updatedData);\n
        scriptEl.textContent = JSON.stringify(merged);\n
    }\n
}\n
\n
async function openVehicleActionModal(carId, action) {\n
    activeModalTriggerElement = document.activeElement;\n
    let car = null;\n
    if (carId === 'new') {\n
        car = {\n
            id: 'new',\n
            kode: 'YZA-UNIT-' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000),\n
            catalog_key: '',\n
            merk: '',\n
            model: '',\n
            tipe: '',\n
            tahun: new Date().getFullYear(),\n
            warna: '',\n
            transmisi: 'automatic',\n
            bahan_bakar: 'bensin',\n
            nomor_rangka: '',\n
            nomor_mesin: '',\n
            kilometer: 0,\n
            harga_beli: 0,\n
            harga_jual: 0,\n
            harga_jual_formatted: 'Rp 0',\n
            kondisi: 'baru',\n
            status: 'tersedia',\n
            is_virtual: false,\n
            preorders: [],\n
            urls: {\n
                store: "http:\/\/127.0.0.1:8000\/admin\/mobil"            }\n
        };\n
    } else {\n
        car = getCarPayload(carId);\n
    }\n
    if (!car) {\n
        alert('Data kendaraan tidak ditemukan.');\n
        return;\n
    }\n
\n
    if (car.urls?.show && ['stock', 'gallery', 'history', 'colors'].includes(action)) {\n
        try {\n
            const response = await fetch(car.urls.show, {headers:{Accept:'application/json'}, cache:'no-store', credentials:'same-origin'});\n
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Stok terbaru belum dapat dimuat.');\n
            const data = await response.json(), state = data.availability || {};\n
            Object.assign(car, {color_stocks:data.color_stocks || [], stock_total:state.stock_total || 0, stock_orderable:state.stock || 0, reserved_units:state.reserved_units || 0});\n
            car.histories = (data.histories || []).map(h => ({tanggal:h.created_at,jenis:h.jenis,jumlah:h.jumlah_perubahan,before_after:h.stock_before+' → '+h.stock_after,catatan:h.catatan}));\n
        } catch (error) { showNotificationToast(error.message, 'error'); return; }\n
    }\n
\n
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('vehicleModalBackdrop');\n
    const dialog = document.getElementById('vehicleModalDialog');\n
    const kicker = document.getElementById('vehicleModalKicker');\n
    const title = document.getElementById('vehicleModalTitle');\n
    const meta = document.getElementById('vehicleModalMeta');\n
    const navTabs = document.getElementById('vehicleModalNavTabs');\n
    const body = document.getElementById('vehicleModalBody');\n
    const footer = document.getElementById('vehicleModalFooter');\n
\n
    // Reset Dialog Classes\n
    dialog.classList.remove('is-compact', 'is-medium', 'is-wide');\n
    navTabs.setAttribute('hidden', '');\n
    navTabs.innerHTML = '';\n
    document.body.classList.add('modal-open');\n
    resetModalFormDirty();\n
\n
    kicker.textContent = 'YZA AUTOMOTIVE · SHOWROOM MANAGEMENT';\n
    const statusText = car.status_label || (car.status ? car.status.toUpperCase() : 'AKTIF');\n
    meta.innerHTML = `<span><strong>${car.kode || 'UNIT BARU'}</strong></span> &bull; <span>${car.merk || 'Mobil'} ${car.model || ''} (${car.tahun || ''})</span> &bull; <span class="badge-status is-${car.status || 'available'}">${statusText}</span>`;\n
\n
    switch (action) {\n
        case 'detail':\n
        case 'specs':\n
            renderDetailAndSpecsModal(car, action, { title, kicker, meta, navTabs, body, footer, dialog });\n
            break;\n
        case 'stock':\n
            renderStockManagementModal(car, { title, kicker, meta, navTabs, body, footer, dialog });\n
            break;\n
        case 'edit':\n
            renderEditVehicleModal(car, { title, kicker, meta, body, footer, dialog });\n
            break;\n
        case 'add_unit':\n
            renderAddUnitModal(car, { title, kicker, meta, body, footer, dialog });\n
            break;\n
        case 'preorder':\n
            renderPreorderModal(car, { title, kicker, meta, body, footer, dialog });\n
            break;\n
        case 'history':\n
            renderStockHistoryModal(car, { title, kicker, meta, body, footer, dialog });\n
            break;\n
        case 'gallery':\n
            renderGalleryAndColorsModal(car, { title, kicker, meta, navTabs, body, footer, dialog });\n
            break;\n
        case 'deactivate':\n
            renderDeactivateConfirmationModal(car, { title, kicker, meta, body, footer, dialog });\n
            break;\n
        default:\n
            console.warn('Unknown action:', action);\n
            return;\n
    }\n
\n
    backdrop.removeAttribute('hidden');\n
    dialog.focus();\n
}\n
\n
function closeVehicleModal(force = false) {\n
    if (!force && isModalFormDirty) {\n
        if (!confirm('Terdapat perubahan formulir yang belum disimpan. Yakin ingin menutup popup?')) {\n
            return false;\n
        }\n
    }\n
    resetModalFormDirty();\n
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('vehicleModalBackdrop');\n
    if (backdrop) {\n
        backdrop.setAttribute('hidden', '');\n
    }\n
    document.body.classList.remove('modal-open');\n
    if (activeModalTriggerElement) {\n
        activeModalTriggerElement.focus();\n
        activeModalTriggerElement = null;\n
    }\n
    return true;\n
}\n
\n
// Global Escape Key Listener for Modals\n
document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {\n
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n
        const backdrop = document.getElementById('vehicleModalBackdrop');\n
        if (backdrop && !backdrop.hasAttribute('hidden')) {\n
            closeVehicleModal();\n
        }\n
    }\n
});\n
\n
// Close when clicking directly on backdrop\n
document.getElementById('vehicleModalBackdrop')?.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
    if (e.target === this) {\n
        closeVehicleModal();\n
    }\n
});\n
\n

// 1 & 4. DETAIL & SPESIFIKASI MODAL (PHASE 3 & 6)\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n

                </div>\n

                    <div style="font-size:22px; font-weight:800; color:#b45309;">${car.reserved_units}</div>\n
                </div>\n
                <div style="padding:14px; background:#f1f5f9; border:1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius:10px; text-align:center;">\n
                    <div style="font-size:11px; color:#475569; font-weight:700;">TERJUAL</div>\n
                    <div style="font-size:22px; font-weight:800; color:#0f172a;">${car.sold_units}</div>\n
                </div>\n

\n
            <div class="modal-info-callout" style="margin-bottom:16px;">\n

                <span>${car.is_virtual ? 'Model ini merupakan showcase resmi katalog APM digital dan saat ini belum memiliki unit fisik tercatat di showroom. Anda dapat mendaftarkan unit fisik baru melalui tombol Tambah Unit.' : `Unit fisik yang tersedia di inventori dealer terdaftar dengan warna resmi: <strong>${car.warna}</strong>.`}</span>\n

\n

                : '<div style="padding:18px; background:#f8fafc; border:1px solid #edf2f7; border-radius:8px; text-align:center; color:#64748b;">Tidak ada pesanan member aktif yang mengikat unit ini saat ini.</div>'}\n
        </div>\n
    `;\n
\n
    function updateDetailFooter(activePaneId) {\n
        if (activePaneId === 'pane-detail-overview') {\n
            dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>\n
                ${!isUserOwner ? (car.is_virtual\n
                    ? `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-primary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal('${car.id}', 'add_unit')" style="background:#059669;">+ Tambah Unit ke Inventori</button>`\n
                    : `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-primary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal('${car.id}', 'edit')">Edit Data Kendaraan</button>`\n
                ) : ''}\n
            `;\n
        } else if (activePaneId === 'pane-detail-specs') {\n
            dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="switchDetailTab('pane-detail-overview')">Lihat Ringkasan</button>\n
            `;\n
        } else if (activePaneId === 'pane-detail-stock') {\n
            dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>\n
                ${!isUserOwner ? (car.is_virtual\n
                    ? `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-primary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal('${car.id}', 'add_unit')" style="background:#059669;">+ Tambah Unit ke Inventori</button>`\n
                    : `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-primary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal('${car.id}', 'stock')">Kelola Stok Kendaraan</button>`\n
                ) : ''}\n
            `;\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    window.switchDetailTab = function(targetId) {\n
        const btn = dom.navTabs.querySelector(`.modal-tab-btn[data-target="${targetId}"]`);\n
        if (btn) btn.click();\n
    };\n
\n
    setupModalPaneTabs(dom.navTabs, dom.body, updateDetailFooter);\n
    updateDetailFooter(initialTab === 'specs' ? 'pane-detail-specs' : 'pane-detail-overview');\n
}\n

// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
// 2. MODAL MANAJEMEN STOK KENDARAAN (PHASE 4)\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
function colorStockEscape(value) { const el = document.createElement('span'); el.textContent = String(value ?? ''); return el.innerHTML; }\n
function renderColorStockCards(car) {\n
    return '<section class="color-stock-summary"><h4>Stok per warna fisik</h4><div class="color-stock-grid">' + (car.color_stocks || []).map(c => '<article class="color-stock-card"><strong><i style="background:' + (/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c.swatch) ? c.swatch : '#cbd5e1') + '"></i>' + colorStockEscape(c.label) + '</strong><span class="color-stock-status">' + (c.stock > 0 ? 'Tersedia' : '× Stok Habis') + '</span><dl><div><dt>Fisik</dt><dd>' + Number(c.stock_total) + '</dd></div><div><dt>Reservasi</dt><dd>' + Number(c.reserved_units) + '</dd></div><div><dt>Ready</dt><dd>' + Number(c.stock) + '</dd></div></dl>' + (c.unverified_units > 0 ? '<small>' + Number(c.unverified_units) + ' stok agregat lama belum terverifikasi</small>' : '') + '</article>').join('') + '</div></section>';\n
}\n
function renderStockManagementModal(car, dom) {\n
    dom.title.textContent = 'Manajemen Stok Kendaraan';\n
    dom.dialog.classList.add('is-medium');\n

        <button type="button" class="modal-tab-btn is-active" data-target="pane-stock-overview" aria-selected="true">Ikhtisar Stok</button>\n
        <button type="button" class="modal-tab-btn" data-target="pane-stock-adjust" aria-selected="false">Penyesuaian Stok</button>\n
    `;\n
\n
    const recentHistories = (car.histories || []).slice(0, 4);\n
\n
    dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
        \n
        <div id="pane-stock-overview" class="modal-pane is-active" style="display:block;">\n
            <div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr); gap:12px; margin-bottom:16px;">\n
                <div style="padding:14px; background:#f8fafc; border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:10px;">\n
                    <div style="font-size:11px; color:#64748b; font-weight:700;">TOTAL STOK FISIK</div>\n
                    <div style="font-size:24px; font-weight:800; color:#0f172a;">${car.stock_total} <small style="font-size:12px; font-weight:500;">unit</small></div>\n
                </div>\n

            </div>\n
\n
            <div class="modal-info-callout" style="margin-bottom:16px;">\n
                <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12.01" y2="8"></line></svg>\n
                <span>Warna fisik unit terdaftar di inventori: <strong>${car.warna || 'Standar'}</strong>. Pengurangan stok tidak diizinkan melebihi ${car.reserved_units} unit yang sedang direservasi.</span>\n
            </div>\n
\n
            <h4 class="section-micro-title">Riwayat Pergerakan Stok Terbaru</h4>\n

                            </tr>\n
                        </thead>\n
                        <tbody>\n
                            ${recentHistories.map(h => `\n
                                <tr>\n
                                    <td style="font-size:11px; color:#64748b; white-space:nowrap;">${h.tanggal}</td>\n
                                    <td><strong>${h.jenis}</strong></td>\n
                                    <td style="text-align:center;"><span style="font-weight:700; color:${h.jumlah.toString().startsWith('-') ? '#dc2626' : '#16a34a'};">${h.jumlah} unit</span></td>\n
                                    <td style="font-family:monospace; font-size:11px;">${h.before_after}</td>\n
                                    <td style="color:#64748b;">${colorStockEscape(h.catatan)}</td>\n
                                </tr>\n
                            `).join('')}\n
                        </tbody>\n
                    </table>\n
                </div>\n
            ` : `\n
                <div style="padding:18px; background:#f8fafc; border:1px solid #edf2f7; border-radius:8px; text-align:center; color:#64748b;">\n
                    Belum ada riwayat pergerakan stok tambahan selain inisialisasi awal.\n
                </div>\n
            `}\n
        </div>\n
\n
        \n
        <div id="pane-stock-adjust" class="modal-pane hidden" style="display:none;">\n
            ${isUserOwner ? `\n
                <div class="modal-warning-callout">\n
                    <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"></line></svg>\n
                    <span>Role Owner hanya memiliki hak akses baca (read-only). Penyesuaian stok dinonaktifkan.</span>\n
                </div>\n
            ` : `\n
                <div class="modal-strip-summary">\n
                    <span>Stok Fisik Saat Ini: <strong>${car.stock_total} unit</strong></span>\n
                    <span>Reservasi Terikat: <strong>${car.reserved_units} unit</strong></span>\n
                    <span>Batas Minimum Stok Baru: <strong>${car.reserved_units} unit</strong></span>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                <form id="stockAdjustForm" onsubmit="submitStockAdjust(event, ${car.id})">\n
                    <input type="hidden" name="_token" value="${document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.content}">\n
\n
                    <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                        <label class="modal-form-label" for="stok_baru">Total Stok Baru <span class="required-mark">*</span></label>\n
                        <input type="number" id="stok_baru" name="stok_baru" class="modal-form-input" min="${car.reserved_units}" value="${car.stock_total}" required>\n
                        <small style="color:#64748b; font-size:11px;">Nilai tidak boleh kurang dari ${car.reserved_units} unit karena sedang direservasi pada pesanan member aktif.</small>\n
                    </div>\n
\n
                    <div id="stockDeltaBox" style="padding:10px 14px; border-radius:8px; background:#f8fafc; border:1px solid #e2e8f0; margin-bottom:14px; font-size:12px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">\n
                        <span>Dampak Kuantitas: <strong id="stockDeltaValue" style="color:#64748b;">0 unit (Tidak ada perubahan)</strong></span>\n
                        <span>Estimasi Siap Jual: <strong id="stockImpactPreview" style="color:#047857;">${car.stock_orderable} unit</strong></span>\n
                    </div>\n
\n
                    <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                        <label class="modal-form-label" for="alasan_preset">Pilihan Alasan Penyesuaian</label>\n
                        <select id="alasan_preset" class="modal-form-select" onchange="onStockReasonPresetChange(this)">\n
                            <option value="">-- Pilih Alasan Penyesuaian Standar --</option>\n
                            <option value="Restock unit baru dari dealer">Restock unit baru dari dealer</option>\n
                            <option value="Rekonsiliasi hasil stock opname fisik gudang">Rekonsiliasi hasil stock opname fisik gudang</option>\n

                            <option value="custom">Alasan khusus lainnya...</option>\n
                        </select>\n
                    </div>\n
\n
                    <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                        <label class="modal-form-label" for="alasan">Keterangan Alasan Resmi <span class="required-mark">*</span></label>\n
                        <input type="text" id="alasan" name="alasan" class="modal-form-input" placeholder="Wajib diisi: Penjelasan penyesuaian inventori" required>\n
                    </div>\n
\n
                    <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                        <label class="modal-form-label" for="catatan">Catatan Audit / Referensi Dokumen</label>\n

                    </div>\n
\n
                    <div class="modal-form-group"><label class="modal-form-label">Bukti / nomor dokumen *</label><input name="bukti" class="modal-form-input" minlength="5" maxlength="500" required></div><div id="stockAdjustError" class="modal-field-error" style="display:none; margin-bottom:12px;"></div>\n
\n
                    <div style="display:none;">\n
                        <button type="submit" id="btnSubmitStockAdjust">Submit</button>\n
                    </div>\n
                </form>\n
            `}\n
        </div>\n
    `;\n
\n
    // Setup live calculation on stock input\n
    const stockInput = dom.body.querySelector('#stok_baru');\n
    const deltaDisplay = dom.body.querySelector('#stockDeltaValue');\n
    const impactPreview = dom.body.querySelector('#stockImpactPreview');\n
    if (stockInput && deltaDisplay) {\n
        const updateDelta = () => {\n
            const val = parseInt(stockInput.value, 10);\n
            if (isNaN(val)) return;\n
            const delta = val - car.stock_total;\n
            const newOrderable = Math.max(0, val - car.reserved_units);\n
            if (delta > 0) {\n
                deltaDisplay.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#16a34a; font-weight:700;">+${delta} unit (Penambahan Stok)</span>`;\n
            } else if (delta < 0) {\n
                deltaDisplay.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#dc2626; font-weight:700;">${delta} unit (Pengurangan Stok)</span>`;\n
            } else {\n
                deltaDisplay.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#64748b; font-weight:600;">0 unit (Tidak ada perubahan)</span>`;\n
            }\n
            if (impactPreview) {\n
                impactPreview.textContent = `${newOrderable} unit`;\n
            }\n
        };\n
        stockInput.addEventListener('input', updateDelta);\n
    }\n
\n
    dom.body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', renderColorStockCards(car));\n
    function updateStockFooter(activePaneId) {\n
        if (activePaneId === 'pane-stock-overview') {\n
            dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal(${car.id}, 'history')">Lihat Riwayat</button>\n
                ${!isUserOwner ? `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-primary" onclick="switchStockTab('pane-stock-adjust')">Sesuaikan Stok</button>` : ''}\n
            `;\n
        } else {\n
            dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="switchStockTab('pane-stock-overview')">Kembali ke Ikhtisar</button>\n
                ${!isUserOwner ? `<button type="submit" form="stockAdjustForm" class="btn-modal-primary" id="btnFooterStockAdjust">Simpan Penyesuaian</button>` : ''}\n
            `;\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    window.switchStockTab = function(targetId) {\n
        const btn = dom.navTabs.querySelector(`.modal-tab-btn[data-target="${targetId}"]`);\n
        if (btn) btn.click();\n
    };\n
\n
    window.onStockReasonPresetChange = function(selectEl) {\n
        const input = document.getElementById('alasan');\n
        if (!input) return;\n
        if (selectEl.value && selectEl.value !== 'custom') {\n
            input.value = selectEl.value;\n
        } else if (selectEl.value === 'custom') {\n
            input.value = '';\n
            input.focus();\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    setupModalPaneTabs(dom.navTabs, dom.body, updateStockFooter);\n
    updateStockFooter('pane-stock-overview');\n
}\n
\n
// Handler Submit Penyesuaian Stok\n
async function submitStockAdjust(e, carId) {\n
    e.preventDefault();\n
    const form = e.target;\n
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnSubmitStockAdjust');\n
    const errBox = document.getElementById('stockAdjustError');\n
    errBox.style.display = 'none';\n
\n
    const car = getCarPayload(carId);\n
    if (!car) return;\n
\n
    btn.disabled = true;\n
    btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan...';\n
\n
    const formData = new FormData(form);\n
\n
    try {\n
        const response = await fetch(car.urls.stock_adjust, {\n
            method: 'POST',\n
            headers: {\n
                'Accept': 'application/json',\n
                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',\n
            },\n
            body: formData,\n
        });\n
\n
        const data = await response.json();\n
        if (response.ok && data.success) {\n
            // Update local payload\n
            const newAvailability = data.availability || {};\n
            updateLocalCarPayload(carId, {\n
                stock_total: newAvailability.stock_total ?? car.stock_total,\n
                stock_orderable: newAvailability.stock ?? car.stock_orderable,\n
            });\n
\n
            // Update row in table\n
            const row = document.getElementById('row-car-' + carId);\n
            if (row) {\n
                const stockCell = row.querySelector('.td-stock-physical strong') || row.querySelector('.td-stock strong');\n
                if (stockCell) stockCell.textContent = (newAvailability.stock_total ?? car.stock_total);\n
                const orderableCell = row.querySelector('.td-stock-ready .stock-ready-badge') || row.querySelector('.td-orderable .orderable-unit-badge');\n
                if (orderableCell) {\n
                    const count = Number(newAvailability.stock || 0);\n
                    orderableCell.textContent = count + ' unit';\n
                    orderableCell.classList.toggle('is-ready', count > 0);\n
                    orderableCell.classList.toggle('is-available', count > 0);\n
                    orderableCell.classList.toggle('is-zero', count <= 0);\n
                    orderableCell.classList.toggle('is-empty', count <= 0);\n
                }\n
                const availBadge = row.querySelector('.td-availability .badge-availability');\n
                if (availBadge) {\n
                    const count = Number(newAvailability.stock || 0);\n
                    if (count > 0) {\n
                        availBadge.className = 'badge-availability is-ready';\n
                        availBadge.textContent = 'Ready';\n
                    } else {\n
                        availBadge.className = 'badge-availability is-exhausted';\n
                        availBadge.textContent = 'Stok Habis';\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            }\n
\n
            closeVehicleModal();\n
            showNotificationToast('Sukses: ' + data.message, 'success');\n
        } else {\n
            errBox.textContent = data.message || 'Terjadi kesalahan saat menyimpan stok.';\n
            errBox.style.display = 'block';\n
        }\n
    } catch (err) {\n
        errBox.textContent = 'Gagal menghubungi server: ' + err.message;\n
        errBox.style.display = 'block';\n
    } finally {\n
        btn.disabled = false;\n
        btn.textContent = 'Simpan Penyesuaian Stok';\n
    }\n
}\n
\n

// Handler Submit Edit Kendaraan\n
async function submitEditVehicle(e, carId) {\n
    e.preventDefault();\n
    const form = e.target;\n
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnSubmitEditVehicle');\n
    const globalErr = document.getElementById('editVehicleGlobalError');\n
    globalErr.style.display = 'none';\n
\n
    // Clear individual field errors\n
    form.querySelectorAll('[data-error-field]').forEach(el => el.textContent = '');\n
\n
    const car = getCarPayload(carId);\n
    if (!car) return;\n
\n
    btn.disabled = true;\n
    btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan...';\n
\n
    const formData = new FormData(form);\n
\n
    try {\n
        const response = await fetch(car.urls.update, {\n
            method: 'POST', // with _method=PUT\n
            headers: {\n
                'Accept': 'application/json',\n
                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',\n
            },\n
            body: formData,\n
        });\n
\n
        const data = await response.json();\n
        if (response.ok && data.success) {\n
            const updated = data.mobil || {};\n
            updateLocalCarPayload(carId, {\n
                merk: updated.merk || car.merk,\n
                model: updated.model || car.model,\n
                tipe: updated.tipe || car.tipe,\n
                tahun: updated.tahun || car.tahun,\n
                warna: updated.warna || car.warna,\n
                kondisi: updated.kondisi || car.kondisi,\n
                status: updated.status || car.status,\n
                harga_jual: updated.harga_jual || car.harga_jual,\n
                harga_jual_formatted: 'Rp ' + Number(updated.harga_jual).toLocaleString('id-ID'),\n
                showcase_slug: updated.showcase_slug || car.showcase_slug,\n
                deskripsi: updated.deskripsi || car.deskripsi,\n
            });\n
\n
            // Update row in table\n
            const row = document.getElementById('row-car-' + carId);\n
            if (row) {\n
                const titleEl = row.querySelector('.car-title');\n
                if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = (updated.merk || car.merk) + ' ' + (updated.model || car.model);\n
                const subtextEl = row.querySelector('.car-subtext');\n
                if (subtextEl) subtextEl.innerHTML = `${updated.tipe || 'Varian Standar'} &bull; ${updated.tahun || car.tahun} &bull; ${updated.warna || car.warna}`;\n
                const priceEl = row.querySelector('.td-price strong');\n
                if (priceEl) priceEl.textContent = 'Rp ' + Number(updated.harga_jual).toLocaleString('id-ID');\n
                const statusBadge = row.querySelector('.td-status .badge-status');\n
                if (statusBadge) {\n
                    const st = (updated.status || car.status).toLowerCase();\n
                    statusBadge.className = 'badge-status is-' + st;\n
                    statusBadge.textContent = st.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + st.slice(1);\n
                }\n
            }\n
\n
            closeVehicleModal();\n
            showNotificationToast('Sukses: ' + data.message, 'success');\n
        } else if (response.status === 422 && data.errors) {\n
            // Validation errors\n
            for (const [field, messages] of Object.entries(data.errors)) {\n
                const errEl = form.querySelector(`[data-error-field="${field}"]`);\n
                if (errEl) {\n
                    errEl.textContent = Array.isArray(messages) ? messages[0] : messages;\n
                }\n
            }\n
            globalErr.textContent = 'Periksa kembali isian formulir yang ditandai merah.';\n
            globalErr.style.display = 'block';\n
        } else {\n
            globalErr.textContent = data.message || 'Gagal menyimpan perubahan kendaraan.';\n
            globalErr.style.display = 'block';\n
        }\n
    } catch (err) {\n
        globalErr.textContent = 'Gagal menghubungi server: ' + err.message;\n
        globalErr.style.display = 'block';\n
    } finally {\n
        btn.disabled = false;\n
        btn.textContent = 'Simpan Perubahan';\n
    }\n
}\n
\n

// 3B. MODAL TAMBAH UNIT FISIK KE INVENTORI (CODEX REFINEMENT)\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
function renderAddUnitModal(car, dom) {\n
    const isNew = car.id === 'new';\n
    dom.title.textContent = isNew ? 'Daftarkan Unit Mobil Baru ke Inventori' : `Tambah Unit Fisik — ${car.merk} ${car.model}`;\n
    dom.kicker.textContent = 'INVENTORI FISIK DEALER · REGISTRASI UNIT';\n
    dom.dialog.classList.add('is-wide');\n
\n
    if (isUserOwner) {\n
        dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
            <div class="modal-warning-callout">\n
                <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"></line></svg>\n
                <span>Role Owner hanya memiliki hak akses baca (read-only). Pendaftaran unit baru dinonaktifkan.</span>\n
            </div>\n
        `;\n
        dom.footer.innerHTML = `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>`;\n
        return;\n
    }\n
\n
    const defaultCode = (isNew || !car.kode || car.is_virtual)\n
        ? ('YZA-' + Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000))\n
        : (car.kode + '-U' + Math.floor(10 + Math.random() * 90));\n
\n
    dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
        <form id="addUnitVehicleForm" onsubmit="submitAddUnit(event, '${car.id}')" autocomplete="off">\n
            <input type="hidden" name="_token" value="${document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.content}">\n
            ${car.showcase_slug ? `<input type="hidden" name="showcase_slug" value="${car.showcase_slug}">` : ''}\n
            <input type="hidden" name="catalog_identity" value="${colorStockEscape(car.catalog_key || (car.id !== 'new' ? 'mobil:' + car.id : ''))}">\n
\n
            <div class="modal-info-callout" style="margin-bottom:16px;">\n
                <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"></line></svg>\n
                <span>Formulir ini mendaftarkan unit fisik kendaraan ke inventori dealer. Seluruh nomor identitas (VIN & Nomor Mesin) wajib diisi valid dan akan diverifikasi keasliannya di sistem.</span>\n
            </div>\n
\n
            <div class="modal-form-grid">\n
                \n
                <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                    <label class="modal-form-label" for="add_unit_merk">Merek Kendaraan <span class="required-mark">*</span></label>\n
                    <input type="text" id="add_unit_merk" name="merk" class="modal-form-input" value="${car.merk || ''}" ${(!isNew && car.merk) ? 'readonly style="background:#f8fafc;"' : 'required placeholder="contoh: Toyota, Lexus, Porsche"'}>\n
                    <div class="modal-field-error" data-error-field="merk" style="display:none;"></div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                    <label class="modal-form-label" for="add_unit_model">Model Kendaraan <span class="required-mark">*</span></label>\n
                    <input type="text" id="add_unit_model" name="model" class="modal-form-input" value="${car.model || ''}" ${(!isNew && car.model) ? 'readonly style="background:#f8fafc;"' : 'required placeholder="contoh: GR Supra, 911 GT3"'}>\n
                    <div class="modal-field-error" data-error-field="model" style="display:none;"></div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                \n
                <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                    <label class="modal-form-label" for="add_unit_tipe">Tipe / Varian</label>\n
                    <input type="text" id="add_unit_tipe" name="tipe" class="modal-form-input" value="${car.tipe || ''}" placeholder="contoh: 3.0L Pro, Performance Edition">\n
                    <div class="modal-field-error" data-error-field="tipe" style="display:none;"></div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                    <label class="modal-form-label" for="add_unit_tahun">Tahun Pembuatan <span class="required-mark">*</span></label>\n
                    <input type="number" id="add_unit_tahun" name="tahun" class="modal-form-input" value="${car.tahun || new Date().getFullYear()}" min="1950" max="${new Date().getFullYear() + 1}" required>\n
                    <div class="modal-field-error" data-error-field="tahun" style="display:none;"></div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                \n
                <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                    <label class="modal-form-label" for="add_unit_warna">Warna Fisik Unit <span class="required-mark">*</span></label>\n
                    <input type="text" id="add_unit_warna" name="warna" class="modal-form-input" value="${car.warna || ''}" placeholder="contoh: Pearl White, Nardo Gray, Midnight Black" required>\n
                    <div class="modal-field-error" data-error-field="warna" style="display:none;"></div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                    <label class="modal-form-label" for="add_unit_kode">Kode Kendaraan (Internal Dealer) <span class="required-mark">*</span></label>\n
                    <input type="text" id="add_unit_kode" name="kode_mobil" class="modal-form-input" value="${defaultCode}" required>\n
                    <div class="modal-field-error" data-error-field="kode_mobil" style="display:none;"></div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                \n
                <div class="modal-form-group">\n
                    <label class="modal-form-label" for="add_unit_vin">Nomor Rangka (VIN Fisik) <span class="required-mark">*</span></label>\n
                    <input type="text" id="add_unit_vin" name="nomor_rangka" class="modal-form-input" style="font-family:monospace; text-transform:uppercase;" placeholder="Contoh: MH3ABC123DK004921" required>\n
                    <div class="modal-field-error" data-error-field="nomor_rangka" style="display:none;"></div>\n
                </div>\n
\n
                <div class="modal-form-group">\n

                        <option value="baru" ${car.kondisi === 'baru' ? 'selected' : ''}>Baru (Brand New / Showroom)</option>\n

    `;\n
\n
    setupFormDirtyTracking(document.getElementById('addUnitVehicleForm'));\n
}\n
\n
async function submitAddUnit(e, carId) {\n
    e.preventDefault();\n
    const form = e.target;\n
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnSubmitAddUnit');\n
    const globalErr = document.getElementById('addUnitGlobalError');\n
    if (globalErr) {\n
        globalErr.style.display = 'none';\n
        globalErr.textContent = '';\n
    }\n
\n
    // Reset inline field errors\n
    form.querySelectorAll('[data-error-field]').forEach(el => {\n
        el.textContent = '';\n
        el.style.display = 'none';\n
    });\n
\n
    btn.disabled = true;\n
    btn.textContent = 'Menyimpan Unit Fisik...';\n
\n
    const formData = new FormData(form);\n
\n
    try {\n
        const response = await fetch("http:\/\/127.0.0.1:8000\/admin\/mobil", {\n
            method: 'POST',\n
            headers: {\n
                'Accept': 'application/json',\n
                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'\n
            },\n
            body: formData\n
        });\n
\n
        const data = await response.json();\n
\n
        if (response.ok && data.success) {\n
            const newCar = data.mobil || {};\n
            resetModalFormDirty();\n
            closeVehicleModal(true);\n
            showNotificationToast(data.message || 'Unit kendaraan berhasil didaftarkan ke inventori.', 'success');\n
\n
            window.location.reload();\n
        } else if (response.status === 422 && data.errors) {\n
            for (const [field, messages] of Object.entries(data.errors)) {\n
                const errEl = form.querySelector(`[data-error-field="${field}"]`);\n
                if (errEl) {\n
                    errEl.textContent = Array.isArray(messages) ? messages[0] : messages;\n
                    errEl.style.display = 'block';\n
                }\n
            }\n
            if (globalErr) {\n
                globalErr.textContent = 'Terdapat data formulir yang belum lengkap atau sudah terdaftar.';\n
                globalErr.style.display = 'block';\n
            }\n
        } else {\n
            if (globalErr) {\n
                globalErr.textContent = data.message || 'Gagal menyimpan unit ke inventori.';\n
                globalErr.style.display = 'block';\n
            }\n
        }\n
    } catch (err) {\n
        if (globalErr) {\n
            globalErr.textContent = 'Gagal menghubungi server: ' + err.message;\n
            globalErr.style.display = 'block';\n
        }\n
    } finally {\n
        btn.disabled = false;\n
        btn.textContent = '+ Simpan Unit ke Inventori';\n
    }\n
}\n
\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
// 3C. MODAL PERMINTAAN PRE-ORDER (CODEX REFINEMENT)\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
function renderPreorderModal(car, dom) {\n
    dom.title.textContent = `Permintaan Pre-Order — ${car.merk} ${car.model}`;\n
    dom.kicker.textContent = 'PRE-ORDER MEMBER · MANAJEMEN ANTREAN UNIT';\n
    dom.dialog.classList.add('is-wide');\n
\n
    const preorders = car.preorders || [];\n
    const stockOrderable = Number(car.stock_orderable || 0);\n
\n
    dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
        <div id="preorderListView">\n
            <div class="preorder-modal-summary-bar">\n
                <div class="summary-stat-box">\n
                    <span class="label">Total Pre-Order Aktif</span>\n
                    <span class="val highlight">${preorders.length} Permintaan</span>\n
                </div>\n
                <div class="summary-stat-box">\n
                    <span class="label">Stok Fisik Siap Jual</span>\n
                    <span class="val ${stockOrderable > 0 ? 'text-green' : 'text-amber'}">\n
                        ${stockOrderable > 0 ? stockOrderable + ' Unit Siap Jual' : '0 Unit (Stok Habis / Belum Ada)'}\n
                    </span>\n
                </div>\n
                <div class="summary-stat-box">\n
                    <span class="label">Model Kendaraan</span>\n
                    <span class="val">${car.merk} ${car.model} (${car.tahun || ''})</span>\n
                </div>\n
            </div>\n
\n
            ${preorders.length === 0 ? `\n
                <div class="preorder-empty-box">\n
                    <svg width="44" height="44" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1.5" style="margin:0 auto 12px; display:block;">\n
                        <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle>\n
                        <polyline points="12 6 12 12 16 14"></polyline>\n
                    </svg>\n
                    <h4 style="margin:0 0 6px; font-weight:700; color:#0f172a; font-size:15px;">Tidak Ada Permintaan Pre-Order Aktif</h4>\n
                    <p style="margin:0 0 16px; font-size:12px; color:#64748b; max-width:440px; margin-inline:auto;">\n
                        Belum ada member yang mengajukan pre-order untuk model <strong>${car.merk} ${car.model}</strong> saat ini.\n
                    </p>\n
                    ${!isUserOwner ? `\n
                        <button type="button" class="btn-action-inline is-primary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal('${car.id}', 'add_unit')">\n
                            + Daftarkan Unit Baru ke Inventori\n
                        </button>\n
                    ` : ''}\n
                </div>\n
            ` : `\n
                <div style="overflow-x:auto;">\n
                    <table class="modal-history-table">\n
                        <thead>\n
                            <tr>\n
                                <th>Kode &amp; Tanggal</th>\n
                                <th>Member &amp; Kontak</th>\n
                                <th>Varian &amp; Warna</th>\n
                                <th style="text-align:center;">Status Pre-Order</th>\n
                                <th style="text-align:center;">Aksi</th>\n
                            </tr>\n
                        </thead>\n
                        <tbody>\n
                            ${preorders.map(po => `\n
                                <tr>\n
                                    <td>\n
                                        <strong style="color:#0f172a;">${po.kode_preorder}</strong>\n
                                        <div style="font-size:11px; color:#64748b;">${po.created_at}</div>\n
                                    </td>\n
                                    <td>\n
                                        <strong style="color:#0f172a;">${po.customer_name}</strong>\n
                                        <div style="font-size:11px; color:#64748b;">${po.customer_code} &bull; ${po.customer_phone}</div>\n
                                    </td>\n
                                    <td>\n
                                        <div style="color:#0f172a; font-weight:600;">${po.variant || 'Standar'} (${po.year})</div>\n
                                        <div style="font-size:11px; color:#64748b;">Warna: <strong style="color:#334155;">${po.color}</strong></div>\n
                                    </td>\n
                                    <td style="text-align:center;">\n
                                        <span class="badge-status is-active" style="text-transform:capitalize;">${(po.status_label || po.status).replace('_', ' ')}</span>\n
                                    </td>\n
                                    <td style="text-align:center;">\n
                                        <button type="button" class="btn-po-review" onclick="viewPreorderDetail(${po.id_preorder}, '${car.id}')" title="Tinjau Permintaan Pre-Order">\n
                                            <svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"></circle><line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65"></line></svg>\n
                                            <span>Tinjau</span>\n
                                        </button>\n
                                    </td>\n
                                </tr>\n
                            `).join('')}\n
                        </tbody>\n
                    </table>\n
                </div>\n
            `}\n
        </div>\n
\n
        <div id="preorderReviewView" style="display:none;">\n
            <div style="margin-bottom:14px;">\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-preorder-back" onclick="switchPreorderView('list')">\n
                    <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><line x1="19" y1="12" x2="5" y2="12"></line><polyline points="12 19 5 12 12 5"></polyline></svg>\n
                    <span>&larr; Kembali ke Daftar Pre-Order</span>\n
                </button>\n
            </div>\n
            <div id="preorderReviewContent"></div>\n
        </div>\n
    `;\n
\n
    dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
        <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>\n
        ${!isUserOwner ? `\n
            <button type="button" class="btn-modal-primary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal('${car.id}', 'add_unit')" style="background:#059669; border-color:#059669;">\n
                + Tambah Unit Fisik untuk Model Ini\n
            </button>\n
        ` : ''}\n
    `;\n
}\n
\n
function switchPreorderView(view) {\n
    const listView = document.getElementById('preorderListView');\n
    const reviewView = document.getElementById('preorderReviewView');\n
    if (!listView || !reviewView) return;\n
\n
    if (view === 'review') {\n
        listView.style.display = 'none';\n
        reviewView.style.display = 'block';\n
    } else {\n
        reviewView.style.display = 'none';\n
        listView.style.display = 'block';\n
    }\n
}\n
\n
async function viewPreorderDetail(preorderId, carId) {\n
    switchPreorderView('review');\n
    const content = document.getElementById('preorderReviewContent');\n
    if (!content) return;\n
\n
    content.innerHTML = `\n
        <div style="padding:40px 20px; text-align:center; color:#64748b;">\n
            <div style="display:inline-block; width:28px; height:28px; border:3px solid #e2e8f0; border-top-color:#dc2626; border-radius:50%; animation:modalSpin 0.8s linear infinite;"></div>\n
            <p style="margin:12px 0 0; font-size:13px; font-weight:600; color:#334155;">Memuat rincian permintaan pre-order #${preorderId}...</p>\n
        </div>\n
    `;\n
\n
    const car = getCarPayload(carId) || {};\n
    const localPo = (car.preorders || []).find(p => p.id_preorder == preorderId) || {};\n
\n
    try {\n
        const response = await fetch('/admin/pre-order-mobil/' + preorderId + '/detail', {\n
            headers: {\n
                'Accept': 'application/json',\n
                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        if (response.ok) {\n
            const data = await response.json();\n
            renderPreorderDetailContent(data, car, preorderId, carId);\n
            return;\n
        }\n
    } catch (e) {\n
        console.warn('Could not fetch server detail, using local preorder payload', e);\n
    }\n
\n
    // Fallback\n
    renderPreorderDetailContent({\n
        preorder: localPo,\n
        customer: {\n
            name: localPo.customer_name,\n
            phone: localPo.customer_phone,\n
            email: localPo.customer_email,\n
            code: localPo.customer_code\n
        }\n
    }, car, preorderId, carId);\n
}\n
\n

\n
    const po = data.preorder || data || {};\n

    const stockOrderable = Number(car.stock_orderable || 0);\n
    const code = data.code || po.kode_preorder || ('PRE-' + String(preorderId).padStart(5, '0'));\n

    const variantName = data.variant || po.varian || po.variant || 'Standar';\n
    const yearVal = data.year || po.tahun || po.year || car.tahun || '—';\n
    const colorVal = data.color || po.warna || po.color || 'Standar';\n

\n

                    <dl class="modal-spec-dl" style="margin:0;">\n
                        <div class="modal-spec-row"><dt>Nama Member</dt><dd><strong>${cust.name || po.customer_name || 'Member YZA'}</strong></dd></div>\n

                </div>\n
\n

                    <dl class="modal-spec-dl" style="margin:0;">\n
                        <div class="modal-spec-row"><dt>Model Kendaraan</dt><dd><strong>${car.merk || ''} ${car.model || ''}</strong></dd></div>\n
                        <div class="modal-spec-row"><dt>Varian / Tipe</dt><dd>${variantName}</dd></div>\n

                    </dl>\n

\n

                        <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; margin-bottom:4px;">\n

                                ${stockOrderable > 0 ? `Unit Fisik Tersedia (${stockOrderable} Unit Siap Jual di Showroom)` : 'Unit Fisik Belum Tersedia di Inventori Operasional'}\n
                            </strong>\n
                        </div>\n
                        <p style="margin:0; font-size:12px; color:${stockOrderable > 0 ? '#047857' : '#b45309'}; line-height:1.5;">\n

                                ? 'Stok fisik kendaraan telah tersedia di dealer. Anda dapat melanjutkan proses ini untuk menerbitkan SPK atau mengabari member bahwa unit telah siap.'\n
                                : 'Saat ini belum ada unit fisik yang siap jual untuk memenuhi pesanan pre-order ini. Daftarkan unit baru ke inventori untuk segera memproses antrean member ini.'}\n

                               </button>`\n
                            : `<button type="button" class="btn-action-inline is-primary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal('${carId}', 'add_unit')">\n
                                <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><line x1="12" y1="5" x2="12" y2="19"></line><line x1="5" y1="12" x2="19" y2="12"></line></svg>\n
                                <span>+ Tambah Unit Fisik</span>\n
                               </button>`\n
                        ) : ''}\n
                    </div>\n
                </div>\n
            </div>\n
\n
            \n
            ${po.catatan ? `\n
                <div style="padding:12px 14px; background:#f8fafc; border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:8px; font-size:12px; color:#475569;">\n
                    <strong>Catatan Khusus dari Member:</strong>\n
                    <p style="margin:4px 0 0; font-style:italic;">"${po.catatan}"</p>\n
                </div>\n
            ` : ''}\n
        </div>\n
    `;\n
}\n
\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
// 5. MODAL RIWAYAT PERGERAKAN STOK (PHASE 7)\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
function renderStockHistoryModal(car, dom) {\n
    dom.title.textContent = 'Riwayat Pergerakan Stok';\n
    dom.dialog.classList.add('is-medium');\n
\n
    const histories = car.histories || [];\n
\n
    if (histories.length === 0) {\n
        dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
            <div style="padding:40px 20px; text-align:center; color:#64748b;">\n
                <svg width="36" height="36" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#94a3b8" stroke-width="1.5" style="margin-bottom:10px;"><ellipse cx="12" cy="5" rx="9" ry="3"></ellipse><path d="M21 12c0 1.66-4 3-9 3s-9-1.34-9-3"></path><path d="M3 5v14c0 1.66 4 3 9 3s9-1.34 9-3V5"></path></svg>\n
                <h4 style="margin:0 0 6px; font-weight:700; color:#0f172a;">Belum Ada Riwayat Pergerakan Stok</h4>\n
                <p style="margin:0; font-size:12px;">Unit kendaraan ini belum memiliki catatan aktivitas inventori tambahan selain inisialisasi awal.</p>\n
            </div>\n
        `;\n
    } else {\n
        dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
            <div style="overflow-x:auto;">\n
                <table class="modal-history-table">\n
                    <thead>\n
                        <tr>\n
                            <th>Waktu</th>\n
                            <th>Aktivitas</th>\n
                            <th style="text-align:center;">Perubahan</th>\n
                            <th>Stok Sebelum &rarr; Sesudah</th>\n
                            <th>Catatan</th>\n
                        </tr>\n
                    </thead>\n
                    <tbody>\n
                        ${histories.map(h => `\n
                            <tr>\n
                                <td style="font-size:11px; color:#64748b; white-space:nowrap;">${h.tanggal}</td>\n
                                <td><strong style="color:#0f172a;">${h.jenis}</strong></td>\n
                                <td style="text-align:center;"><span style="font-weight:700; color:${h.jumlah.toString().startsWith('-') ? '#dc2626' : '#16a34a'};">${h.jumlah} unit</span></td>\n
                                <td style="font-family:monospace; font-size:11px;">${h.before_after}</td>\n
                                <td style="color:#64748b;">${colorStockEscape(h.catatan)}</td>\n
                            </tr>\n
                        `).join('')}\n
                    </tbody>\n
                </table>\n
            </div>\n
        `;\n
    }\n
\n
    dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
        <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>\n
        <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal(${car.id}, 'stock')">Kelola Stok</button>\n
    `;\n
}\n
\n

// 6. MODAL GALERI FOTO & WARNA KENDARAAN (PHASE 8)\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
function renderGalleryAndColorsModal(car, dom) {\n

    dom.navTabs.removeAttribute('hidden');\n
    dom.navTabs.innerHTML = `\n
        <button type="button" class="modal-tab-btn is-active" data-target="pane-gallery-photos" aria-selected="true">Foto Kendaraan</button>\n
        <button type="button" class="modal-tab-btn" data-target="pane-gallery-catalog-colors" aria-selected="false">Warna Katalog</button>\n
        <button type="button" class="modal-tab-btn" data-target="pane-gallery-inventory-color" aria-selected="false">Warna Unit Inventori</button>\n
    `;\n
\n
    const angles = car.angles || {};\n
    const photos = car.photos || [];\n
    const colors = car.colors || [];\n
    const uploadedAnglesCount = Object.values(angles).filter(a => a.path).length;\n
\n
    dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
        \n

                            <span class="badge-dot ${item.path ? 'is-active' : 'is-inactive'}"></span>\n
                        </div>\n
                    </div>\n

            </div>\n
\n
            <div class="uploaded-photos-header">\n
                <h4 class="section-micro-title" style="margin:0;">Galeri Foto Tambahan (${photos.length})</h4>\n
                ${!isUserOwner ? `\n
                    <form action="${car.urls.photo_store}" method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data" class="quick-upload-form">\n
                        <input type="hidden" name="_token" value="${document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.content}">\n

            <h4 class="section-micro-title" style="margin-top:0;">Daftar Pilihan Warna Showroom (Klik untuk Rincian)</h4>\n
            <div class="color-swatches-list" style="margin-bottom:16px;">\n
                ${colors.length > 0\n
                    ? colors.map((w, idx) => `\n
                        <div class="color-chip is-interactive ${w.is_default ? 'is-default' : ''}" data-color-idx="${idx}">\n

                        </div>\n
                    `).join('')\n
                    : '<div style="padding:16px; background:#f8fafc; border:1px solid #edf2f7; border-radius:8px; color:#64748b; font-size:12px;">Tidak ada master warna katalog showroom terpisah. Model menggunakan warna dasar bawaan.</div>'}\n
            </div>\n

            <div id="catalogColorDetailPanel" style="margin-top:12px;"></div>\n

                <span>Warna Fisik Unit yang tercatat pada inventori operasional saat ini adalah: <strong style="font-size:14px; text-transform:uppercase;">${car.warna || 'Tidak Ditentukan'}</strong></span>\n
            </div>\n

                    <div>Tipe Inventori: <strong>${car.reserved_units > 0 ? 'Batch Fisik dengan Reservasi Mengikat' : 'Stok Fisik Tersedia'}</strong></div>\n
                </div>\n
            </div>\n
\n
            <div style="padding:12px 16px; background:#f8fafc; border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:10px; font-size:12px; color:#64748b; line-height:1.6;">\n
                <strong>Kebijakan Alokasi Dealer:</strong> Seluruh transaksi pesanan SPK dan reservasi unit secara otomatis mengikat unit fisik dengan warna <strong>${car.warna}</strong>. Permintaan warna showroom lain yang belum memiliki stok fisik dialokasikan melalui proses pemesanan ulang (pre-order).\n

\n
    // Interactive helper: Set gallery preview image\n
    window.setGalleryActivePreview = function(url) {\n

\n

            swatchChips.forEach((c, i) => c.classList.toggle('is-selected', i === idx));\n

            if (!w) return;\n
            detailPanel.innerHTML = `\n
                <div class="modal-section-box" style="margin-top:10px;">\n
                    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; margin-bottom:8px;">\n

                            ${w.is_default ? '<span class="badge-primary-photo">Warna Utama Katalog</span>' : '<span style="font-size:11px; color:#64748b; background:#f1f5f9; padding:4px 8px; border-radius:6px;">Pilihan Tambahan</span>'}\n
                        </div>\n
                    </div>\n
                    <div style="font-size:12px; color:#475569; margin-bottom:10px;">\n
                        Status Showroom: <strong style="color:#16a34a;">Aktif pada Konfigurator Member</strong>\n
                    </div>\n
                    <div style="padding:10px 14px; background:#ffffff; border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:8px; font-size:11px; color:#64748b;">\n
                        <em>Informasi: Pilihan warna katalog memungkinkan member memilih tampilan showroom yang diinginkan. Ketersediaan stok fisik sebenarnya ditampilkan pada tab <strong>Warna Unit Inventori</strong>.</em>\n
                    </div>\n
                </div>\n
            `;\n
        };\n
\n
        swatchChips.forEach((chip, i) => {\n
            chip.addEventListener('click', () => selectColor(i));\n
        });\n
        selectColor(0);\n
    }\n
\n
    dom.body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', renderColorStockCards(car));\n
    function updateGalleryFooter(activePaneId) {\n
        if (activePaneId === 'pane-gallery-photos' || activePaneId === 'pane-gallery-catalog-colors') {\n
            dom.footer.innerHTML = `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>`;\n
        } else if (activePaneId === 'pane-gallery-inventory-color') {\n
            dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
                <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>\n
                ${!isUserOwner ? `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-primary" onclick="openVehicleActionModal(${car.id}, 'edit')">Edit Data Kendaraan</button>` : ''}\n
            `;\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    setupModalPaneTabs(dom.navTabs, dom.body, updateGalleryFooter);\n
    updateGalleryFooter('pane-gallery-photos');\n
}\n
