// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
// 7. MODAL KONFIRMASI NONAKTIFKAN KENDARAAN (PHASE 9)\n
// -------------------------------------------------------------\n
function renderDeactivateConfirmationModal(car, dom) {\n
    dom.title.textContent = 'Konfirmasi Nonaktifkan Kendaraan';\n
    dom.dialog.classList.add('is-compact');\n
\n
    if (isUserOwner) {\n
        dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
            <div class="modal-warning-callout">\n
                <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"></line></svg>\n
                <span>Role Owner hanya memiliki hak akses baca (read-only). Nonaktifkan kendaraan dinonaktifkan.</span>\n
            </div>\n
        `;\n
        dom.footer.innerHTML = `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>`;\n
        return;\n
    }\n
\n
    if (car.is_sold) {\n
        dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
            <div class="modal-warning-callout">\n
                <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"></line></svg>\n
                <span>Kendaraan dengan status <strong>Terjual</strong> tidak dapat dinonaktifkan karena telah selesai diserahterimakan ke pelanggan.</span>\n
            </div>\n
        `;\n
        dom.footer.innerHTML = `<button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Tutup</button>`;\n
        return;\n
    }\n
\n
    dom.body.innerHTML = `\n
        <div class="modal-warning-callout">\n
            <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="m21.73 18-8-14a2 2 0 0 0-3.48 0l-8 14A2 2 0 0 0 4 21h16a2 2 0 0 0 1.73-3Z"></path><line x1="12" y1="9" x2="12" y2="13"></line><line x1="12" y1="17" x2="12.01" y2="17"></line></svg>\n
            <div>\n
                <strong>Perhatian: Tindakan Penonaktifan</strong>\n
                <p style="margin:4px 0 0; font-size:11px;">Kendaraan <strong>${car.merk} ${car.model} (${car.kode})</strong> akan disembunyikan dari katalog showroom publik.</p>\n
            </div>\n
        </div>\n
\n
        ${car.reserved_units > 0 ? `\n
            <div style="padding:10px 14px; background:#fffbeb; border:1px solid #fde68a; border-radius:8px; color:#92400e; font-size:12px; margin-bottom:14px;">\n
                <strong>Peringatan Reservasi:</strong> Terdapat ${car.reserved_units} reservasi/pesanan aktif pada kendaraan ini. Data pesanan member tetap aman dan tidak akan terhapus.\n
            </div>\n
        ` : ''}\n
\n
        <p style="font-size:12px; color:#475569; line-height:1.5;">\n
            Menonaktifkan kendaraan tidak akan menghapus data historis, riwayat stok, atau transaksi sebelumnya. Status dapat diaktifkan kembali sewaktu-waktu melalui menu Edit.\n
        </p>\n
\n
        <form id="deactivateVehicleForm" onsubmit="submitDeactivateVehicle(event, ${car.id})">\n
            <input type="hidden" name="_token" value="${document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.content}">\n
            <input type="hidden" name="_method" value="PATCH">\n
            <div id="deactivateVehicleError" class="modal-field-error" style="display:none; margin-top:8px;"></div>\n
        <div class="modal-form-group" style="grid-column:1/-1"><label class="modal-form-label">Alasan perubahan stok *</label><input name="alasan_stok" class="modal-form-input" minlength="5" maxlength="160" required></div><div class="modal-form-group" style="grid-column:1/-1"><label class="modal-form-label">Bukti / nomor dokumen verifikasi *</label><input name="bukti_stok" class="modal-form-input" minlength="5" maxlength="500" placeholder="Nomor surat jalan / berita acara pemeriksaan" required></div></form>\n
    `;\n
\n
    dom.footer.innerHTML = `\n
        <button type="button" class="btn-modal-secondary" onclick="closeVehicleModal()">Batal</button>\n
        <button type="submit" form="deactivateVehicleForm" class="btn-modal-danger" id="btnSubmitDeactivate">Konfirmasi Nonaktifkan</button>\n
    `;\n
}\n
\n
// Handler Submit Nonaktifkan Kendaraan\n
async function submitDeactivateVehicle(e, carId) {\n
    e.preventDefault();\n
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnSubmitDeactivate');\n
    const errBox = document.getElementById('deactivateVehicleError');\n
    errBox.style.display = 'none';\n
\n
    const car = getCarPayload(carId);\n
    if (!car) return;\n
\n
    btn.disabled = true;\n
    btn.textContent = 'Memproses...';\n
\n
    const formData = new FormData(e.target);\n
\n
    try {\n
        const response = await fetch(car.urls.deactivate, {\n
            method: 'POST', // with _method=PATCH\n
            headers: {\n
                'Accept': 'application/json',\n
                'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',\n
            },\n
            body: formData,\n
        });\n
\n
        const data = await response.json();\n
        if (response.ok && data.success) {\n
            updateLocalCarPayload(carId, { status: 'nonaktif' });\n
\n
            // Update row in table\n
            const row = document.getElementById('row-car-' + carId);\n
            if (row) {\n
                const statusBadge = row.querySelector('.td-status .badge-status');\n
                if (statusBadge) {\n
                    statusBadge.className = 'badge-status is-inactive';\n
                    statusBadge.textContent = 'Nonaktif';\n
                }\n
            }\n
\n
            closeVehicleModal();\n
            showNotificationToast('Kendaraan berhasil dinonaktifkan.', 'success');\n
        } else {\n
            errBox.textContent = data.message || 'Gagal menonaktifkan kendaraan.';\n
            errBox.style.display = 'block';\n
        }\n
    } catch (err) {\n
        errBox.textContent = 'Gagal menghubungi server: ' + err.message;\n
        errBox.style.display = 'block';\n
    } finally {\n
        btn.disabled = false;\n
        btn.textContent = 'Konfirmasi Nonaktifkan';\n
    }\n
}\n
\n
// Helper: Setup Tab Switching inside Modal\n
function setupModalPaneTabs(navTabs, body, onTabChange = null) {\n
    const tabBtns = navTabs.querySelectorAll('.modal-tab-btn');\n
    const panes = body.querySelectorAll('.modal-pane');\n
\n
    tabBtns.forEach(btn => {\n
        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
            const targetId = this.getAttribute('data-target');\n
            tabBtns.forEach(b => {\n
                b.classList.remove('is-active');\n
                b.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'false');\n
            });\n
            this.classList.add('is-active');\n
            this.setAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');\n
\n
            panes.forEach(p => {\n
                if (p.id === targetId) {\n
                    p.classList.remove('hidden');\n
                    p.classList.add('is-active');\n
                    p.style.display = 'block';\n
                } else {\n
                    p.classList.add('hidden');\n
                    p.classList.remove('is-active');\n
                    p.style.display = 'none';\n
                }\n
            });\n
\n
            // Reset scroll position to top on tab switch\n
            body.scrollTop = 0;\n
\n
            if (typeof onTabChange === 'function') {\n
                onTabChange(targetId);\n
            }\n
        });\n
    });\n
}\n
\n
// Helper: Simple Notification Toast\n
function showNotificationToast(msg, type = 'success') {\n
    let container = document.getElementById('vehicleActionToastContainer');\n
    if (!container) {\n
        container = document.createElement('div');\n
        container.id = 'vehicleActionToastContainer';\n
        container.style.cssText = 'position:fixed; bottom:24px; right:24px; z-index:100000; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:8px;';\n
        document.body.appendChild(container);\n
    }\n
\n
    const toast = document.createElement('div');\n
    toast.style.cssText = 'padding:12px 18px; border-radius:10px; background:#0f172a; color:#ffffff; font-size:13px; font-weight:600; box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.25); display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; animation:modalSlideUp 180ms ease-out;';\n
    toast.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#10b981; font-size:16px;">✓</span> <span>${msg}</span>`;\n
    container.appendChild(toast);\n
\n
    setTimeout(() => {\n
        toast.style.opacity = '0';\n
        toast.style.transition = 'opacity 200ms ease';\n
        setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 250);\n
    }, 3200);\n
}\n

    <script>\n
        (() => {\n
            const gate = document.querySelector('[data-trade-in-gate]');\n
            const triggers = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-trade-in-trigger]')];\n
            if (!gate || !triggers.length) return;\n
            const dialog = gate.querySelector('.trade-in-gate-dialog');\n
            let returnFocus = triggers[0];\n
            const close = () => { gate.hidden = true; document.body.style.overflow = ''; returnFocus?.focus(); };\n
            const open = (event) => { event.preventDefault(); gate.hidden = false; document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; dialog.focus(); };\n
            triggers.forEach((trigger) => trigger.addEventListener('click', (event) => { returnFocus = trigger; open(event); }));\n
            gate.querySelectorAll('[data-trade-in-close]').forEach((button) => button.addEventListener('click', close));\n
            document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => { if (!gate.hidden && event.key === 'Escape') close(); });\n
        })();\n
    </script>\n
    <script>\n
        (() => {\n
            const panel = document.querySelector('[data-detail-vehicle-id]');\n
            const heart = panel?.querySelector('[data-detail-favorite]');\n
            if (panel && heart) {\n
                const ids = [...new Set([String(panel.dataset.detailVehicleId || ''), window.location.pathname].filter(Boolean))];\n
                const key = 'yza_favorite_cars';\n
                const read = () => { try { const value = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key) || '[]'); return Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(String) : []; } catch (error) { return []; } };\n
                const write = (value) => { try { localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value)); } catch (error) {} };\n
                const render = (active) => { heart.textContent = active ? '♥' : '♡'; heart.classList.toggle('is-favorited', active); heart.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(active)); heart.setAttribute('aria-label', `${active ? 'Hapus' : 'Tambahkan'} Audi R8 ${active ? 'dari' : 'ke'} favorit`); };\n
                render(read().some((favorite) => ids.includes(favorite)));\n
                heart.addEventListener('click', () => {\n
                    const favorites = read();\n
                    const active = !favorites.some((favorite) => ids.includes(favorite));\n
                    const next = active\n
                        ? [...new Set([...favorites, ...ids])]\n
                        : favorites.filter((favorite) => !ids.includes(favorite));\n
                    write(next);\n
                    render(active);\n
                });\n
            }\n

            const thumbnailsRoot = document.getElementById('detail-thumbnails');\n

            const caption = document.getElementById('detail-gallery-caption');\n
            const swatches = [...document.querySelectorAll('.detail-swatch')];\n
            const transitionImage = (src, alt) => {\n
                mainImage.classList.add('is-switching');\n
                // Update immediately so an old rear image can never overwrite the\n
                // newly selected color after a delayed animation callback.\n
                mainImage.src = src || fallbackImage;\n
                mainImage.alt = alt;\n
                window.requestAnimationFrame(() => mainImage.classList.remove('is-switching'));\n
            };\n
            const renderGallery = () => {\n
                const gallery = galleryData[state.color] || galleryData.default;\n
                const images = gallery.images || [];\n
                if (!images.length) return;\n
                state.index = Math.min(state.index, images.length - 1);\n
                const image = images[state.index];\n
                state.selectedColor = state.color;\n
                state.selectedAngle = image.type;\n
                transitionImage(image.src, `${image.label} R8`);\n
                counter.textContent = `${String(state.index + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / ${String(images.length).padStart(2, '0')}`;\n
                caption.textContent = `${image.label} · YZA Automotive`;\n
                thumbnailsRoot.innerHTML = images.map((item, index) => `<button class="detail-thumbnail ${index === state.index ? 'is-active' : ''}" type="button" data-index="${index}" role="listitem" aria-label="Lihat ${item.label}"><img src="${item.src}" alt="${item.label} R8"><span>0${index + 1}</span></button>`).join('');\n
            };\n
            const changeColor = (swatch) => {\n
                state.color = swatch.dataset.colorKey;\n
                // A color change starts at the first image of the selected variant.\n
                // This prevents the previous variant's active angle from leaking into the new set.\n
                state.index = 0;\n
                swatches.forEach((item) => { const active = item === swatch; item.classList.toggle('is-active', active); item.setAttribute('aria-pressed', active ? 'true' : 'false'); });\n
                colorLabel.textContent = swatch.dataset.color;\n
                if (specColor) specColor.textContent = swatch.dataset.color;\n
                const color = galleryData[state.color];\n
                document.querySelector('[data-public-color-stock]').textContent = color.stock > 0 ? color.stock + ' unit siap jual' : 'Stok Habis';\n
                const action = document.querySelector('[data-public-color-action]');\n
                const preorder = new URL("http:\/\/127.0.0.1:8000\/pelanggan\/pre-order\/create", location.origin);\n
                preorder.searchParams.set("slug", "audi-r8");\n
                preorder.searchParams.set('warna', color.label);\n
                action.href = color.purchase_url || preorder.toString(); action.textContent = color.stock > 0 ? 'Lanjutkan Pembelian' : 'Ajukan Pre-Order';\n
                renderGallery();\n
            };\n
            swatches.forEach((swatch) => swatch.addEventListener('click', () => changeColor(swatch)));\n
            thumbnailsRoot.addEventListener('click', (event) => {\n
                const thumbnail = event.target.closest('.detail-thumbnail');\n
                if (!thumbnail) return;\n
                state.index = Number(thumbnail.dataset.index);\n
                renderGallery();\n
            });\n
            document.querySelector('.detail-gallery-arrow-prev')?.addEventListener('click', () => { const count = (galleryData[state.color]?.images || []).length; if (!count) return; state.index = (state.index - 1 + count) % count; renderGallery(); });\n
            document.querySelector('.detail-gallery-arrow-next')?.addEventListener('click', () => { const count = (galleryData[state.color]?.images || []).length; if (!count) return; state.index = (state.index + 1) % count; renderGallery(); });\n
            mainImage.addEventListener('error', () => { if (mainImage.src !== fallbackImage) { mainImage.src = fallbackImage; } });\n
            renderGallery();\n
            const recommendationRoot = document.querySelector('[data-detail-recommendations]');\n
            const recommendationViewport = recommendationRoot?.querySelector('[data-recommendation-viewport]');\n
            const recommendationStep = () => recommendationViewport ? Math.max(280, recommendationViewport.clientWidth * (window.innerWidth <= 600 ? .84 : window.innerWidth <= 1000 ? .52 : .27)) : 0;\n
            recommendationRoot?.querySelector('[data-recommendation-prev]')?.addEventListener('click', () => recommendationViewport?.scrollBy({ left: -recommendationStep(), behavior: 'smooth' }));\n
            recommendationRoot?.querySelector('[data-recommendation-next]')?.addEventListener('click', () => recommendationViewport?.scrollBy({ left: recommendationStep(), behavior: 'smooth' }));\n
        })();\n
